## 2026-10-21T23:10:00Z — skel-platform — the tester's hand-in (contract)
- 50 test(s), one per behaviour; sealed: tests/contract/skel_platform/test_skelp_config.py, tests/contract/skel_platform/test_skelp_identity.py, tests/contract/skel_platform/test_skelp_llm.py, tests/contract/skel_platform/test_skelp_log.py, tests/contract/skel_platform/test_skelp_storage.py
- a test for every invariant of the contract: I1, I2, I3, I4
- question to the contract Q1: Backoff `min(2**k, 30) * (0.5..1.0 jitter)`: does k start at 0 for the first retry (first delay 0.5-1 s) or at 1 (first delay 1-2 s)? — reading taken: k = 0 for the first retry: delays in [0.5,1.0] then [1.0,2.0]. Undisputed tests only bound delays by the union of both readings and the 30 s cap.
- question to the contract Q2: Usage(prompt_tokens, output_tokens, thought_tokens) from the SDK's usage_metadata: is output_tokens candidates_token_count alone, or candidates + thoughts? (The contract names the fields, not the mapping.) — reading taken: prompt_tokens = prompt_token_count, output_tokens = candidates_token_count, thought_tokens = thoughts_token_count; the same three values in the llm_call line. Undisputed tests check only prompt_tokens
- question to the contract Q3: `list(bucket, prefix)`: is prefix a plain string prefix over full paths (Cloud Storage semantics, so 'u1/ca' matches 'u1/cards.json' and 'u1' matches 'u10/…'), recursive into sub-folders, and are '' and a trailing '/' lawful prefixes? The path rule (empty segment -> ValueError, I3) would refuse 'u1/ — reading taken: GCS semantics in both implementations: plain string prefix, recursive; '' and 'u1/' accepted. Undisputed tests use only prefix 'u1' with no 'u10…' file and no nested folder, and only prefixes with '..
- question to the contract Q4: `load_settings(environ)` with `environ` injected: must it read ONLY that mapping, or may pydantic-settings also pick up THINKHOW_* from the process environment (the BaseSettings default)? — reading taken: Only the given environ; process THINKHOW_* variables are ignored. Undisputed config tests clear THINKHOW_* from the process env.
- question to the contract Q5: «в environ є K_SERVICE або CLOUD_RUN_JOB»: does a key present with an empty value count as present? — reading taken: Yes, presence of the key is enough. I4's test uses non-empty values only.
- question to the contract Q6: `id` = part of `sub` after the last ':' — when `sub` has no ':' at all, is the id the whole sub, or is it an IdentityError? — reading taken: The whole sub (rsplit semantics), then the regex check.
- question to the contract Q7: `Done` «з повним сирим JSON»: is raw_json exactly the text as it arrived (chunks joined, whitespace kept), or any text that parses to the same JSON? — reading taken: Exactly the joined text. Undisputed tests compare json.loads of raw_json only.

- question to the contract Q6: NothingToRetry → 409: the contract gives no human text for it. — reading taken: Only status 409 and one <p class="error"> fragment are asserted.

The undisputed tests use a non-empty unknown command.
- question to the contract Q2: `analyze_failed` (`uid`, `topic_id`, `session_no`, клас винятку; без тексту): does «без тексту» mean without the exception's message, or only without the conversation's text? — reading taken: Undisputed test: no conversation text (turn texts) in any log line. - question to the contract Q3: Under which key does the exception's class go in `analyze_failed`? The contract names the keys uid, topic_id, session_no but not the class's. — reading taken: Any string value of the line equals the class name (`ValidationError`, `LlmUnavailable`); no key is required — both readings pass, so the tests are not disputed.
- question to the contract Q4: Topic-level `analyze_failed` «без `session_no`»: the key absent, or present with null? — reading taken: Either: `line.get("session_no") is None` — not disputed.
- question to the contract Q5: `[Сесія <session_no> закрита <closed_at ISO>]`: which ISO form — `+00:00` or `Z`, with or without fractional seconds? And are blank lines allowed between the turn lines and this line? — reading taken: Any value `datetime.fromisoformat` reads back to closed_at; blank lines are ignored; the non-blank lines must be exactly the turn lines then the closed line — not disputed.
- question to the contract Q6: The observation file's bytes are written as «…»: model_dump_json, json.dumps of model_dump, indented? — reading taken: Any JSON that `Observation.model_validate_json` accepts; the parsed Observation is compared — not disputed.

- question to the contract Q2: I2 checks the pairs 'у `:root` і в блоці `prefers-color-scheme: dark`'. Must the dark block redeclare every variable, or does a variable it leaves out keep its `:root` value, as the cascade gives? — reading taken: The cascade: the dark palette is `:root` with the dark block's declarations laid over it. A variable missing from both is a failure.
- question to the contract Q3: The contract does not say how palette values are written. How are values with alpha, or written with `color-mix()`, `light-dark()` or system colours, measured for contrast? — reading taken: var() chains are followed. - question to the contract Q4: Outside the palette, does a literal fallback inside `var(--x, #fff)` count as a literal colour? — reading taken: No. The whole `var(...)`, fallback included, counts as taking the value through the palette.
- question to the contract Q5: The ban on literal colours names `color`, `background`, `background-color`, `border-color`, `outline-color` and the shorthands `border`, `border-left` 'тощо'. Are per-side longhands such as `border-left-color` covered too? — reading taken: Only the listed properties plus the shorthands are checked: `border`, `border-top/right/bottom/left`, `border-block(-start/-end)`, `border-inline(-start/-end)`, `outline`. Longhands like `border-left-
- question to the contract Q6: '`white-space: pre-wrap` для `.turn .text` і `.turn.model`': must those exact selectors appear, or does an equivalent selector such as `p.turn.model` or `.turn > .text` do? — reading taken: Each of the two selectors appears, whitespace normalised, in the selector list of some rule anywhere in the sheet that declares `white-space: pre-wrap`.
- question to the contract Q7: 'вміст сторінки — у `<main>`': which elements count as the page's content? — reading taken: On «Мої теми», the new-topic form (`action=/topics`). On the topic page, `#t1`, `#t2`, `#send`, `#live` and every `<h1>`. Exactly one `<main>` per page.

- a test for every invariant of the contract: I1, I2, I3, I4, I5
- question to the contract Q1: `local_found`: «queries=(текст репліки до 80 символів,)». Is a longer turn cut to exactly its first 80 characters (`text[:80]`), or may it be stripped of trailing whitespace / cut at a word / end with an ellipsis? — reading taken: Exactly the first 80 characters, nothing stripped or added. Shorter turns are taken whole (that part is in the sealed test_rq_local.py).
- question to the contract Q2: Sources are deduplicated «за `url`, у порядку першої появи». When the same url comes twice with different titles (or once with a title and once without), whose title is kept — the first appearance's or a later one? — reading taken: Not decided by a test: every repeated url in the tests carries the same title.
- question to the contract Q3: «`title` — `web.title`, а без нього — `web.domain`, без обох — `url`». Does an empty string title ("") count as «без нього» (falls back to domain), or only an absent/None title? — reading taken: Not decided by a test: only absent (None) titles and domains are used.
- question to the contract Q4: `lead` gives «`ResearchEvent(depth="quick", …)`». Are `question` and `report` of a quick event always None, or may `question` carry e.g. the person's turn? — reading taken: Not fixed: lead and real-chain tests do not compare `question`/`report` (workspace tests that build the event themselves do check they are copied into research.json).

- question to the contract Q6: `local_script` «на таку репліку додає в `map_updates` пункт типу `fact`»: also for a schema without `reply` (update_schema), where `local_found` gives None? — reading taken: Not decided by a test: only answer_schema is used.
- question to the contract Q7: A `Found` that is not None but has neither queries nor sources (possible from a `found_script` of ScriptedLlm, not from Vertex): does `lead` give a ResearchEvent? — reading taken: Not decided by a test.
- NOT SEALED until its question is answered: tests/contract/research_quick/test_rq_disputed_q1_local_found_first_80.py (Q1)
